# Held-out test: the tournament winner against every rival (seeds 31-60)

Generated 2026-09-24T09:47:47.279Z (15:17 IST). Roster roster_3000_seed42, horizon 2026-10-01 to 2026-12-15, world seeds 31-60 (30), every run through the CLI arena path (runArena, world roster id roster_3000_seed42). All numbers are on synthetic data (PUCAR's generated roster and a simulated court calibrated to PUCAR's tables).

**The pick: g237** (archive g237), registered as `benchtime_final`. Daily list: one priority index per case (value of a hearing today plus the waiting cost it stops, less wasted trips, per minute), filled to 111% of the day with a 30% standby list. Next dates: the earliest day with room after PUCAR's gap (failed or unheard matters sooner). Process desk: matters whose summons, notice or warrant is not back are handled at the desk. No check-in. Every 4+ year case before the bench at least every 45 working days.

Tournament record: failed 0 guardrails on the decision seeds 7-20 and 1 on the confirmation seeds 21-30.

Pre-registered: a guardrail failure on 31-60 is reported here and the pick does not change. Guardrails follow final rule, 14:15 (after adversarial review): paired non-inferiority against status_quo_60, passing when the 95% t bound of (candidate minus today) clears the margin; t uses 29 degrees of freedom (t = 2.045); the tournament script's table uses 1.96 from 20 degrees of freedom, shown in the last column.

- ADP: /tmp/variants/adp/adp.patch report only (/tmp/heldout-adp); status_quo_60 re-run there on the first two seeds to check the court is identical.
- Identity check: benchtime_final (registered) equals the winner's genome run as a zoo cell on seeds 31, 32: identical, every measure.
- Identity check: status_quo_60 in the ADP temp copy equals the package's on seeds 31, 32: identical, every measure.

#### Definitions

| Field | Measure | Better | Source and computation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| readme.utilisation | Utilisation | higher | Repo README, "What you're scored on": "% of available court minutes actually spent on hearings that were reached". Minutes of substantive and failed hearings / (capacity x sitting days); not capped, so an overrun day can push it past 100%. |
| readme.reachRate | Reach rate | higher | Repo README, "What you're scored on": "% of what you scheduled the court actually gets to before time runs out". Reached / listed, listed = substantive + failed + not reached + court not sitting (vacated and desk matters were never on the day's list). |
| readme.substantiveness | Substantiveness | higher | Repo README, "What you're scored on": "% of reached hearings that move the case forward". Substantive / reached. |
| readme.backlog4yHeardShare | 4y+ cases heard at all | higher | Repo README, "What you're scored on": "% of the oldest cases (4+ years) that get heard at all". Cases aged 4+ years at the start with at least one reached hearing / all cases aged 4+ at the start. |
| readme.backlog4ySubstantiveShare | 4y+ cases heard substantively | higher | DESIGN.md companion to the README backlog measure: cases aged 4+ at the start with at least one substantive hearing / all such cases. |
| readme.predictabilityGapDays | Gap from first scheduled to first heard | lower | Repo README, "What you're scored on": "avg. gap, in days, between a case's first scheduled date and when it's actually heard". Mean over heard cases of (first reached date - first date the case came up in the horizon); never-heard cases are excluded here and counted in extra.predictabilityGapDaysCensored. |
| caseStudy.utilisation | Utilisation within capacity | higher | Case study, section 6: "Is the judge's day well packed, without being overbooked or leaving significant capacity unused?" Sum over sitting days of min(capacity, reached minutes) / (capacity x sitting days). |
| caseStudy.overrunDays | Days that overran | lower | Case study, section 6: "without being overbooked". Sitting days whose used minutes exceeded capacity (or that the simulator marked as overrun). |
| caseStudy.idleMinutesShare | Idle capacity | lower | Case study, section 6: "leaving significant capacity unused". Sum over sitting days of max(0, capacity - minutes used) / (capacity x sitting days). |
| caseStudy.heldAsScheduled | Held as scheduled (as listed) | higher | Case study, section 6: "Are cases actually heard when they are listed?" Rows reached on the date they were listed for (promised date, or the listing date when no promise) / (listed + deferred). Always shown next to the all-due and substantive readings. |
| caseStudy.heldAsScheduledAllDue | Held as scheduled (all due) | higher | Case study, section 6, reported both ways per DESIGN.md: same numerator / every row that fell due (listed + deferred + vacated + desk + due on a closure day), so matters kept off the bench count against the schedule. |
| caseStudy.substantiveness | Substantiveness | higher | Case study, section 6: "If a case is heard, does it meaningfully advance towards judgment or resolution?" Substantive / reached. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.0-1 | Cases aged 0-1 years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.1-3 | Cases aged 1-3 years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.3-4 | Cases aged 3-4 years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.4-5 | Cases aged 4-5 years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.5+ | Cases aged 5+ years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.3+ | Cases aged 3+ years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsStart.4+ | Cases aged 4+ years at the start |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the start. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.0-1 | Cases aged 0-1 years at the end |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.1-3 | Cases aged 1-3 years at the end |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.3-4 | Cases aged 3-4 years at the end |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.4-5 | Cases aged 4-5 years at the end |  | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.5+ | Cases aged 5+ years at the end | lower | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.3+ | Cases aged 3+ years at the end | lower | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.ageBandsEnd.4+ | Cases aged 4+ years at the end | lower | Case study, section 6: "does it reduce the number of cases sitting in the 5+ year buckets?" Pending cases by age band (365.25-day years since filing) at the end. |
| caseStudy.nextDateExcessDays | Next date excess over the minimum | lower | Case study, section 6: "does the system recommend a sensible and efficient next date?"; ask 5: "Match the gap to the actual procedural minimum". Mean over reached, non-disposing hearings of max(0, days to the next date - PUCAR's reference gap for that hearing type). |
| caseStudy.wastedRelistShare | Next dates the case was not ready for | lower | DESIGN.md reading of Case study, section 6 next-date quality: "next dates on which the case was not ready". Among relisted dates that revealed readiness (substantive, failed, desk, vacated), the share handled at the desk, vacated, or failed for awaiting process, external dependency, not ready or sought time. A desk row whose previous row for the case was also a desk row is left out: the registry re-checking a return, with nobody called (extra.wastedRelistShareInclDeskRechecks keeps it). |
| siddarth.throughputPerMonth | Throughput (disposals per month) | higher | Siddarth's slide 06: "Throughput = cases disposed per month". Headline disposals in the horizon (verdict, settlement, compounding, acquittal for the complainant's default, dismissal for steps not taken) / horizon length in months (calendar days / 30.44). Post-judgment closures and long-pending splits are in extra.throughputPerMonthAllRoutes. |
| siddarth.judgeTimeUsed | Judge time used | higher | Siddarth's slide 06: "Judge time used = minutes used / minutes available". Sum of the day's minutes used / (capacity x sitting days). |
| siddarth.wastedListings | Wasted listings | lower | Siddarth's slide 06: "Wasted listings = (not reached + adjourned) / listed". (Not reached + court not sitting + failed) / listed. |
| siddarth.heldOnPromisedDate | Held on the promised date | higher | Siddarth's slide 06: "Predictability = hearings held on the promised date". Rows reached on their promised date / rows that carried a promised date (every promise that fell due, however it ended). |
| siddarth.oldestPendingAgeYears | Oldest pending case | lower | Siddarth's slide 06: "Fairness = age of the oldest pending cases". Maximum age at the end among cases still pending. |
| siddarth.p95PendingAgeYears | 95th percentile pending age | lower | Siddarth's slide 06 fairness, DESIGN.md: p95 of age at the end among pending cases. |
| siddarth.neverHeard | Cases never heard | lower | Siddarth's slide 06 fairness, DESIGN.md: "cases never heard". Roster cases with no reached hearing in the horizon. |
| siddarth.loadBalanceCv | Load balance (CV of daily minutes) | lower | Siddarth's slide 06: "Load balance = spread of minutes across judges"; with one judge, the coefficient of variation (population sd / mean) of minutes used per sitting day. |
| extra.listedPerDay | Listed per sitting day |  | DESIGN.md extra: listings per day. |
| extra.reachedPerDay | Reached per sitting day | higher | Substantive + failed per sitting day. |
| extra.substantivePerDay | Substantive per sitting day | higher | Substantive hearings per sitting day. |
| extra.disposed | Disposals (headline) | higher | DESIGN.md extra: cases disposed in the horizon by verdict, settlement, compounding, acquittal for the complainant's default, or dismissal for steps not taken. Post-judgment closures and long-pending splits are counted apart, below. |
| extra.disposed4yPlus | Disposals of 4y+ cases (headline) | higher | DESIGN.md extra: headline disposals of cases aged 4+ at the start. |
| extra.tripsPerSubstantive | Trips per substantive hearing | lower | DESIGN.md extra, case study ask 1 (minimise trips): parties and advocates who came to listed matters (recorded attendance; all four when not called) / substantive hearings. |
| extra.wastedTripShare | Wasted trips | lower | Trips to listed matters that were not substantive / all trips. |
| extra.deskPerDay | Desk matters per sitting day |  | Rows handled at the process desk per sitting day. |
| extra.vacatedPerDay | Vacated per sitting day |  | Rows vacated at the day-before check-in per sitting day. |
| extra.deferredPerDay | Deferred per sitting day | lower | Due matters the policy neither listed nor sent to the desk (broken promises) per sitting day. |
| extra.sittingDays | Sitting days |  | Working days in the horizon on which the court sat. |
| extra.overrunMinutes | Minutes past capacity | lower | Sum over sitting days of max(0, minutes used - capacity). |
| extra.reachRateExclStandby | Reach rate excluding uncalled standby | higher | Alternative reading of the README reach rate: uncalled standby matters removed from the denominator. |
| extra.reachRateSittingDays | Reach rate on sitting days | higher | Alternative reading of the README reach rate: listings on closure days removed from the denominator. |
| extra.predictabilityGapDaysCensored | Gap to first heard, never heard at the end | lower | Alternative reading of the README predictability: cases scheduled but never heard count with their gap to the horizon end. |
| extra.casesScheduled | Cases whose date came up |  | Distinct cases with any row in the horizon. |
| extra.promisesHonouredInclDesk | Dates honoured (court or desk order) | higher | Siddarth's slide 06 predictability, read as a magistrate reads it: rows reached on their promised date, or taken up at the desk that day with process or a report still out ("process not returned, issue fresh, call on X" honours the date), / rows that carried a promised date. |
| extra.promisesBroken | Dates broken or never given | lower | Promised dates on which the court passed no order (not reached, deferred, vacated, court not sitting), plus cases given no date inside the horizon at all, so parking a case past the horizon counts against the policy. |
| extra.heldOnPromisedShareOfHeard | Heard rows that were on the promised date | higher | Alternative reading of Siddarth's predictability: rows reached on their promised date / all reached rows. |
| extra.nextDateGapDays | Mean next date gap |  | Mean calendar days from a reached, non-disposing hearing to the next date given. |
| extra.nextDateShortShare | Next dates inside the minimum |  | Share of next dates given sooner than PUCAR's reference gap for the hearing type. |
| extra.wastedRelistShareInclAbsence | Next dates wasted, counting absences | lower | Alternative reading of wastedRelistShare that also counts failures for a party's absence (desk re-checks left out, as in the headline). |
| extra.wastedRelistShareInclDeskRechecks | Next dates not ready, counting desk re-checks | lower | The earlier reading of wastedRelistShare: every desk row after a case's first row counts as a wasted relist, including a desk row straight after another desk row. |
| extra.neverHeard4yPlus | 4y+ cases never heard | lower | Cases aged 4+ at the start with no reached hearing. |
| extra.pending4yPlusEnd | Pending 4y+ cases at the end | lower | Pending cases aged 4+ at the end (same as ageBandsEnd 4+). |
| extra.disposalsInferred | Disposals inferred from the log |  | 1 when the simulator did not mark disposing rows and disposals were inferred (substantive JUDGEMENT, or a substantive hearing of a post-judgment case); settlements are then missed. Averaged across runs, the share of runs inferred. |
| extra.heldSubstantiveAllDue | Substantive on the promised date (all due) | higher | The substantive reading of Case study, section 6 "Are cases actually heard when they are listed?": substantive rows on the date they were listed for / every row that fell due (the all-due denominator). A hearing that is called and adjourned does not count as held. |
| extra.firstPromiseKept | First promise kept, per case | higher | Per-case reading of Siddarth's slide 06 predictability: among cases whose first row in the horizon fell on its promised date, the share reached that day. |
| extra.nextDateExcessNextPurposeDays | Next date excess over the gap for the next purpose | lower | The case study's reading of ask 5: PUCAR's gap is "time to next hearing given this is the purpose", so the next date is scored against the gap for the purpose it was given for. Mean of max(0, days to the next date - that gap); the purpose is the case's next row, or when that falls beyond the horizon, the same purpose after a failed hearing and the next compulsory stage after a substantive one. |
| extra.nextDateNextPurposeInferredShare | Next purposes inferred |  | Share of next dates in nextDateExcessNextPurposeDays whose purpose was inferred because the case's next row fell beyond the horizon. |
| extra.nextDatesBeyondHorizonShare | Next dates beyond the horizon end |  | Share of next dates given after reached, non-disposing hearings that fall after the horizon end; those hearings are not seen again inside the run. |
| extra.predictabilityGapFromStartDays | Gap from the horizon start to first heard, all cases | lower | Alternative reading of the README predictability: every roster case, from the horizon start to its first reached hearing, or to the horizon end when never heard. |
| extra.minutesWaited | Minutes waited per person heard | lower | Case study ask 1 (a court day that does not waste people's time): for each party or advocate present at a reached hearing, bench minutes from the call time given (10:30 when none) to the minute the hearing started. Needs start minutes in the log; n/a otherwise. |
| extra.minutesWaitedInclNotReached | Minutes waited, counting matters not reached | lower | As minutesWaited, also counting people present at matters not reached, who waited until the court rose (the day's reached minutes, or capacity if more). |
| extra.oldest100PendingMeanAgeYears | Mean age of the 100 oldest pending cases | lower | Siddarth's slide 06 fairness, "age of the oldest pending cases", read over the oldest hundred: the single oldest age barely moves between policies over a short horizon. Mean age at the end of the 100 oldest pending cases. |
| extra.neverActedOn | Cases never acted on | lower | Cases with neither a reached hearing nor a desk action in the horizon: the fairness gap without counting pointless calls or ignoring desk work. Siddarth's slide: who is never heard? |
| extra.backlog4yActedOnShare | 4y+ cases acted on | higher | Cases aged 4+ at the start that were heard, or had a desk action taken on their process, / all such cases. |
| extra.disposedVerdict | Disposals by verdict |  | Cases disposed by judgment (conviction or acquittal on the merits); part of the headline. |
| extra.disposedSettlement | Disposals by settlement |  | Cases settled, typically on a mediation report; part of the headline. |
| extra.disposedCompounded | Disposals by compounding |  | Offences compounded (s.147 NI Act); part of the headline. |
| extra.disposedAcquittedDefault | Acquittals for the complainant's default |  | Accused acquitted because the complainant did not appear; part of the headline. |
| extra.disposedDismissedSteps | Dismissals for steps not taken |  | Complaints dismissed because the complainant did not take steps (process fee, address); part of the headline. |
| extra.disposedPostJudgment | Post-judgment closures |  | Cases already decided whose remaining matter was closed; off the file, but not in the headline. |
| extra.disposedLpSplit | Long-pending splits |  | Cases split off to the long-pending register (accused absconding); off the file, but not in the headline. |
| extra.disposedAllRoutes | Cases off the file, all routes |  | Headline disposals plus post-judgment closures and long-pending splits. |
| extra.disposedRouteInferred | Disposals with an inferred route |  | Disposals whose row carried no route, classified as a post-judgment closure for a case already decided, a settlement on a REPORTS hearing, and a verdict otherwise. |
| extra.throughputPerMonthAllRoutes | Throughput, all routes |  | Cases off the file by any route / horizon length in months. |
